<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — My first test of Seedance 2.5! ❤️‍🔥 conclusions: Resolution and realistic quality have improved significantly. The sound is clean and cinematic. Using a 30-second duration is...</summary>

~~~~text
My first test of Seedance 2.5! ❤️‍🔥

conclusions:

Resolution and realistic quality have improved significantly. The sound is clean and cinematic.

Using a 30-second duration is risky.

When you ask for many specific elements within 30 seconds, you increase the likelihood of errors.

I used a prompt with eight references, and it couldn't capture all eight references within the 30-second timeframe.

I created it with @capcutapp

Main prompt in the comments.
~~~~

~~~~text
High-quality 3D animated cartoon, stylized modern family-animation film

look, oversized heads, big expressive eyes, exaggerated proportions,

smooth rounded clay-like 3D surfaces, subsurface scattering skin, vibrant

saturated colors, cinematic animated lighting. NOT realistic, NOT live

action.

Single continuous 30-second shot, no cuts, one flowing camera move.

SUBJECT: a young woman with an oversized round head, huge expressive

eyes, dark hair in a messy bun, a long mustard-yellow raincoat, boots, a

small satchel. Same design, hair, and outfit throughout, no drift.

LOCATION: a cozy stylized night market in the rain, narrow covered lane,

strings of warm hanging bulbs, steaming food stalls with rounded cartoon

awnings, friendly cartoon vendors and shoppers moving around her, glossy

wet cobblestones reflecting the lights, puddles everywhere.

COLOR 60:30:10 — 60% deep blue rainy night and wet stone / 30% warm

golden bulb and stall light / 10% accent of her mustard-yellow raincoat.

0–8s: the camera tracks backward ahead of her as she walks through the

market with a small bounce in her step, dodging puddles, steam curling

from stalls, vendors waving as she passes.

8–16s: the camera arcs smoothly around to her side, revealing the full

depth of the market behind her, then rises to follow just over her

shoulder as she stops to smile at a stall of glowing paper lanterns.

16–24s: she turns down a quieter narrow alley, the camera following, the

crowd noise falling away, single bulbs swinging gently overhead, her

reflection rippling through a puddle as she steps over it.

24–30s: the camera cranes up and pulls back into a wide as she reaches a

warmly lit doorway at the alley's end, pauses, shakes the rain off her

coat, and steps inside. Hold on the glowing doorway.

PHYSICS: raindrops bounce and splash with cartoon weight, her coat swings

and ripples as she walks, puddles ripple outward from each step, steam

rises and curls, hanging bulbs sway gently.

AUDIO: rain pattering on awnings, cheerful market chatter, sizzling food,

her boots splashing, the noise thinning to soft rain in the alley, a warm

door chime at the end.

FORBIDDEN: no cuts, no design drift in face, hair, or coat, no realistic

or live-action rendering, no auto-generated subtitles, no background

music, no readable text or logos, no named studio references, no warping

geometry.
~~~~

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a 30 second cinematic short film titled The Suit Was Still Listening, presented in 16:9 with synchronized dialogue, environmental sound, and original music. The film must...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a 30 second cinematic short film titled The Suit Was Still Listening, presented in 16:9 with synchronized dialogue, environmental sound, and original music. The film must feel completely live action, physically grounded, and captured on location, never glossy or synthetic.

Visual language: a rain soaked neighborhood laundromat at midnight, warm amber fluorescent tubes inside, cold blue street light and moving car reflections outside, wet pavement, fogged windows, chipped enamel machines, baskets, detergent boxes, loose receipts, realistic skin texture, natural eye moisture, subtle fabric lint, imperfect hair, restrained film grain, gentle halation, lifted shadows, rich but believable color. Wardrobe creates bold editorial color blocking: Mara wears a cobalt trench over a cream blouse, Noah wears a rust knit polo, the teenage attendant wears faded green workwear, and an elderly customer wears a pale gray suit and dark hat. Use expressive medium close shots, tactile inserts, occasional low angles, clean profile compositions, and brief handheld movement that feels operated by a human. Preserve consistent faces, clothing, props, screen direction, reflections, hand anatomy, and spatial continuity. Use realistic speech timing, breathing, blinking, eye focus, and precise lip synchronization.

0 to 3 seconds. Exterior wide shot through falling rain. A lonely laundromat glows on a dark corner. A black sedan turns slowly into the street. Camera makes a subtle forward creep. Music begins with muted upright bass, brushed snare, a low analog pulse, and the rhythmic churn of washers.

3 to 6 seconds. Interior macro shot through a round washer door. A red scarf turns through soap and water. Cut to Mara opening a dryer. A tiny black transmitter falls from the lining of a cream suit and strikes the tile with a sharp metallic click. The score briefly drops out.

6 to 10 seconds. Low medium two shot. Noah freezes beside a folding table. He says quietly, You said clean the suit. Mara studies the transmitter in her palm and replies, I did. You hid a heartbeat in the lining. Keep the delivery controlled and intimate, not theatrical.

10 to 14 seconds. Tight insert on the teenage attendant checking an old signal meter beside the change machine. A green needle jumps. He looks toward the fogged front window and says, It is still transmitting. Camera racks focus from the meter to white headlights sliding across the glass.

14 to 18 seconds. The sedan stops outside. The elderly customer continues folding a shirt without looking up. He says, Then he is already here. Hold on Mara as the moving headlights carve across her face. Let silence, rain, and machine motors carry the tension.

18 to 22 seconds. Noah whispers, Back door. The old man calmly turns the deadbolt on the back exit and answers, Too obvious. Mara notices a delivery rider outside pushing a rolling laundry cart past the window. Use a fast sequence of close shots: her eyes, the transmitter, the cart wheel, the sedan mirror.

22 to 27 seconds. Mara crosses naturally behind a row of machines, slips the transmitter into a sealed laundry bag on the moving cart through an open service hatch, and returns without drawing attention. Noah asks, What did you do. Mara watches the cart continue down the wet street and says, Gave him a cleaner story.

27 to 30 seconds. Exterior telephoto view. The black sedan pulls away and follows the cart. Cut back inside to a symmetrical medium shot of the four characters framed by spinning washer doors. The red scarf circles behind Mara like a slow warning light. She sits opposite Noah and says, Now tell me who owns the suit. End on the transmitter signal fading from the attendant meter as the final bass note lands.

Sound design must include rain on glass, distant tires on wet road, washer motors, dryer buttons, fabric movement, the transmitter click, door lock, fluorescent hum, and soft room reflections around every voice. Dialogue must remain clean and naturally mixed above the music. Avoid visual distortion, artificial camera acceleration, floating objects, excessive shallow focus, oversharpening, plastic skin, extra fingers, unreadable signage, random background motion, or dreamlike effects.
~~~~

</details>

<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — 30-second cinematic sequence. 1977 suburban Boston during a zombie outbreak. Use the uploaded husband, wife and male character as the exact character references. Authentic Kodak...</summary>

~~~~text
30-second cinematic sequence.

1977 suburban Boston during a zombie outbreak. Use the uploaded husband, wife and male character as the exact character references. Authentic Kodak Eastmancolor photochemical cinema with warm golden afternoon sunlight, subtle halation, fine 35mm film grain, Panavision anamorphic lenses, practical effects only, realistic 1970s production design.

The sequence opens with an elderly zombie brutally attacking a terrified man inside a narrow residential alley. The zombie bites the man's neck while he desperately struggles to escape. This attack dominates the foreground. Far behind them, softly blurred through a long telephoto lens, the husband, wife and the uploaded male character suddenly sprint across the street from left to right between abandoned 1970s cars, ignoring the attack as they desperately flee the spreading outbreak.

The perspective naturally changes to a smooth side tracking shot following the three survivors running together through burning suburban streets. Their formation constantly changes. Sometimes the uploaded male character leads, then the husband overtakes him, while the wife briefly runs in front before falling back again. Their movement feels exhausted, frightened and realistic rather than heroic. Thick smoke drifts across the road as elderly zombies slowly emerge from burning houses and narrow residential streets behind them.

The camera later observes the chase from behind a burning vintage sedan. Orange flames fill the foreground while the three survivors suddenly burst through thick smoke in the background before disappearing behind abandoned vehicles. The camera gently pans to keep them inside the frame as more zombies continue appearing throughout the neighborhood.

The sequence then reveals another layered composition. Two elderly zombies violently struggle over a fallen victim in the foreground while, farther behind them, the husband, wife and uploaded male character weave through abandoned cars, burning debris and drifting smoke without stopping. Every layer of the frame remains active with environmental storytelling.

The wife briefly stumbles while avoiding burning debris. The husband immediately catches her arm and pulls her forward without slowing down. The uploaded male character looks back, realizes several zombies are getting closer, then accelerates and leads the group toward a safer street.

The perspective suddenly changes into a perfect 90-degree bird's-eye view directly overhead. The camera looks completely vertical over an entire suburban intersection. The three survivors run diagonally through streets, sidewalks and front yards, constantly changing formation while dozens of elderly zombies slowly surround them from every direction. Burning rooftops, abandoned vintage cars, collapsed wooden fences, smoke, autumn trees and spreading fire create a striking geometric composition viewed from directly above. The camera slowly rises higher while maintaining the exact overhead angle, revealing the true scale of the outbreak.

The sequence ends with a wide elevated cinematic view. The camera slowly pulls backward as the three survivors become tiny figures still running through the burning neighborhood while countless zombies wander across roads, front yards and houses beneath the warm golden afternoon sky.

Audio features a tense cinematic orchestral score with deep cellos, low brass, restrained percussion, rising strings and subtle analog synthesizer textures, blended with realistic footsteps, heavy breathing, crackling fire, distant screams, wind, zombie groans and neighborhood ambience.

Authentic 1977 American cinema. Rich foreground, midground and background storytelling. Practical filmmaking. No HDR. No CGI. No digital sharpening. No modern objects.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
ジャパニーズホラーの空気感。彩度を落とした病的な緑と灰色のアンダートーンによるカラーグレーディング。腰から上のトラッキングショットのワンカット長回し。青いストライプのボタンダウンシャツにカーキのチノパンを着た少年が、霧に沈んだ静まり返った日本の田舎の集落の通りを走っている。少年の顔は静かな恐怖に震え、目には今にもこぼれそうな涙が光り、口は声にならない嗚咽をこらえるように薄く開いている。カメラはゆっくりとした意図的な手持ちの動きで少年に寄り添うように漂う。集落は不気味なほど静か。濃い白い霧が家々の間に低く垂れ込め、すべての音を吸い込んでいる。通りのあちこちに、苔・蔦・絡まった緑の葉で全身が覆われた人型の存在が、ゆっくりと硬直したゾンビのような歩き方で、濡れた路面に足を引きずりながら歩いている。その動きは機械的で不自然。一部は少年が通り過ぎると顔のない苔の頭をゆっくりと回し、別の個体は不気味な遅延動作で蔦の腕を伸ばす。音楽はなし、濡れた足音とかすかな水滴の音だけ。少年は通りの植物人間をすり抜け、木造家屋の間の狭い路地へ入る。カメラは薄暗い通路を追う。路地にも数体の植物人間が同じ遅いゾンビ歩きでのろのろと移動しており、苔むしたシルエットが薄暗い中で黒い木の壁と見分けがつかない。少年は脇をすり抜け、必死に日本家屋の引き戸を押し開けて中に入る。カメラは室内へ続く——薄暗い和風建築の内部が一瞬映る。板張りの廊下がまっすぐ奥へ伸び、両側の部屋は開いた襖の向こうに畳の間が見え、壁には黒カビのシミ、あらゆる表面に湿気が光り腐敗を暗示する。廊下や部屋の中を植物人間が同じ硬直したゾンビ歩きでゆっくりと移動している。苔の体がほぼ暗闇の中で蠢く。少年はすぐに奥の出口を見つける——廊下の突き当たりの木の戸。軋む板張りの廊下を駆け抜ける。廊下にいた植物人間がゆっくりと体を回し、少年が通り過ぎる際に苔の腕を伸ばす。少年は裏手の戸を引き開け、家の裏の灰色の曇天の下に広がる畑へとよろめき出る。カメラは地面近くまで下がり、ニンジンの葉や大根の葉の列の間から向こう側を覗く。背景に少年が畑を横切って走る姿が霧の中にぼんやり見える。長く居心地の悪い間。やがて手前の葉が震え始める——激しくではなく、ゆっくりと忍び寄るような震え。暗く湿った土の中から、実物と同じサイズの大根・ニンジン・カブが、腕や脚のような根の形をして音もなく地面から這い出してくる——どれも拳ひとつ分から前腕ほどの実際の野菜の大きさ。最初はゆっくりと現れ、青白い体から土がぼろぼろと崩れ落ち、やがて速度を上げて土の上をよちよちと歩き出す。囁きと叫びの間のような細く甲高いキーキー音を発する。畑のあらゆる畝から数百体が姿を現す。カメラがゆっくりと上がり少年を捉える。少年は立ち止まっている。足元を見下ろす。表情が困惑から純粋な恐怖へと変わる。小さな根菜たちが足元に群がり、何十本ものニンジン・白い大根・丸いカブが靴にしがみつきズボンの裾をよじ登る。少年は震えながら振り払おうとするが、四方八方からさらに押し寄せる——キーキー鳴く小さな野菜の潮流が黒い土の上を流れるように少年へ向かってくる。少年は膝をつく。カメラがゆっくりと安定した軌道で少年を中心に回り始める。Jホラー映画のような冷静な客観性をもって。根菜たちが細い鳴き声の合間の沈黙の中で積み重なっていく——胴体をよじ登り、腕をつかみ、肩と背中に群がる。少年の嗚咽が空っぽの畑に響く。少年は仰向けに倒れ込み、まだ弱々しく根菜を押し返そうとしている。カメラは急がない軌道で回り続け、根菜が層を成して少年を埋めていく——脚が消え、胸が覆われ、腕が飲み込まれる。叫びがくぐもり、やがて無音になる。青いストライプシャツの最後の一片が、数百体の小さな野菜が蠢く塚の下に消える。カメラが緩やかにやや高い位置へと上昇する——真俯瞰ではなく、軽いハイアングルで脈打つ根菜の塚を見下ろす。この視点から畑の全体が四方に広がる。あらゆる畝、あらゆる溝から、無数の小さな根菜——ニンジン、大根、カブ——が根の足で土から出てきて、中央の塚に向かって静かに途切れることなく行進を続けている。果てしない、音のない行列。音響：濡れた足音、水滴、木の軋み、かすかな風、遠い囁きのように層を成す細い甲高い鳴き声、土が静かに裂ける音、少年の叫びがくぐもった沈黙へと消えていく、土の上を根が這う静かなざわめき。
~~~~

</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — 15-second photorealistic cinematic video, 16:9, with synchronized stereo audio. A female explorer with short black hair, a red waterproof jacket, and a gray backpack remains...</summary>

~~~~text
15-second photorealistic cinematic video, 16:9, with synchronized stereo audio.

A female explorer with short black hair, a red waterproof jacket, and a gray backpack remains perfectly consistent inside an abandoned Victorian greenhouse during a nighttime storm.

0–4s: A low-angle tracking shot follows her walking across the wet floor. Her boot hits a puddle, creating a physically accurate splash.

4–8s: Without a cut, the camera moves into a medium side-tracking shot. She pushes aside a wet branch as a brass mechanical hummingbird emerges, circles her head, and casts warm golden light across her face.

8–12s: The camera performs a fluid 120-degree orbit. She extends her hand and the hummingbird lands precisely on her finger. Its wings slow naturally as water droplets fall from its metallic body. She says in Spanish, with natural lip sync: “Así que eras real.”

12–15s: Lightning illuminates the greenhouse. A rapid rack focus reveals an enormous bioluminescent flower opening in the background and releasing glowing blue particles. The explorer and hummingbird turn toward it simultaneously. End with a slow push toward the flower.

35mm anamorphic cinematography, natural depth of field, realistic physics, coherent reflections, detailed skin, blue storm light contrasted with the hummingbird’s golden glow.

Immersive stereo rain, wind, vibrating glass, synchronized footsteps and splashes, spatial mechanical buzzing, clean dialogue, and one deep musical note when the flower opens.

Priorities: absolute character and wardrobe consistency, stable hummingbird design, correct anatomy, natural hands, precise physical contact, coherent movement, consistent lighting, and continuous camera motion. Exactly one explorer and one hummingbird. No subtitles, text, logos, or additional characters.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
30秒电影级青春竞速剧情短片,2d动画风格。主角是一个位少年车手,驾驶机车参加高规格比赛。整体风格热血、青春、情感强烈、电影感强,具有完整起承转合和清晰情绪弧线。全片 仅使用两种运镜:高速跟拍、慢动作环绕。台词极少,自然像记忆碎片一样出现,语气真挚、温柔、克制,不喊口号,不过度熔情。不要灾难感、不要负向表达、不要夸张科幻。重点突出青春竞 速中的爱、支撑、反击与成长。0秒到5秒黄昏赛道,高速激烈竞速开场。镜头紧贴地面高速跟拍少年机车,轮胎掠过赛道边线,机车轰鸣,风声凌厉,气氛紧张炽热。少年全神贯注,夕阳在车 身金属外壳上拉出锋利高光。5秒到9秒进入关键弯道后、少年被对手突然超越。高速跟拍继续推进,画面表现出排名下滑和节奏被打乱的压迫感。头盔内近景出现短暂失神、呼吸发紧、轻微动 摇。少年低声说:"CanIstill catchup..."9秒到14秒少年落在后方,呼吸加重,情绪跌入低谷。比赛没有停止,机车仍在高速前进。画面开始在高速骑行中闪回温暖记忆碎片:童年学车时,有 人从身后扶住他;父亲替他整理头盔,动作细致而安静;终点前,-一个温柔注视他的笑容;黄昏坡道上,并肩走过的背影。这些记忆都用金色逆光、柔和慢动作、碎片化感受呈现。14秒到18秒 音乐从压抑逐渐转向昂扬。记忆中传来克制而温柔的声音:"Don't be afraid - I'm always here.""Stay steady. And locokforward."少年眼神重新聚焦,呼吸慢慢稳定,情绪从动接转为坚定。18 秒到23秒少年重新找回信念,全力提速,精准切线反击。高速跟拍白表现机车压弯、出弯、逼近前车的力量感与控制力。少年低声而坚定地说:"Iwon't stophere."23秒到27秒前方出现一段上 扬赛道,少年迎着夕阳全速冲刺。画面只保留呼吸声、引擎声和持续抬升的音乐,不再加入多余台词。机车借助惯性腾空而起,进入震撼慢动作。记忆深处最后传来一句带笑的温柔声音:"Goo n."27秒到30秒摄影机围绕半空中的机车进行慢动作环绕特写。将热血、温柔、自由、向上飞跃的情绪推向高潮。身后开出繁花特效,随后出现seedance,参考 @图像1
~~~~
